EXPANDED 2D CENTERED BANANA: HMC TUNING
========================================================================================
Full Cartesian search. No short screening, halving, or automatic boundary expansion.
Tuning only: these are not held-out results or evidence of a final win.
143 settings; 10 blocks; 4 chains per block.
Each chain: 1000 warmup + 10000 retained draws at every setting.
Same target, base mass, physical starts, observables and tuning seeds across methods.
The number of settings differs: equal per-setting evaluation, not equal total tuning compute.
IAT: all chains in a block; biased autocovariances with denominator N at every lag;
Geyer initial-positive pairs, cumulative minima; no floor or manual lag cutoff.
R-hat: rank-normalized split and folded split maximum. Moment errors and R-hat are descriptive, not gates.
Block efficiency=1/(actual retained target gradients per draw * worst physical-observable IAT).
Score=geometric mean of every block efficiency. Undefined IAT or any nonfinite trajectory makes the score undefined.
Cost counts newly executed retained target gradients, excludes warmup, and does not double-count cached gradients.
Valid full trajectories cost L new target gradients per draw; inner THC substeps call no target gradient.
Gradient cost is not wall-clock cost.
Ordinary HMC, fixed M0, exact Gaussian momentum refresh, leapfrog and Metropolis.
Original per-method exact-tie selection rules are retained. No diagnostic eligibility gates.
